+function [manifestTable, qualityTable] = audit_case( ...
+        definition, specification, domain, generationRuntimeS)
+% AUDIT_CASE Build v5 manifest/quality rows from one in-memory mesh domain.
+
+    legacyAuditDefinition = phase32.definition();
+    [manifest, ~] = phase32.audit_domain(legacyAuditDefinition, ...
+        specification, domain, generationRuntimeS);
+    polar = domain.fs.quality_controlled_polar;
+    truncation = domain.cfg.outer_truncation.effective;
+    manifest.topology_mode = string(definition.topologyMode);
+    manifest.requested_near_radial_step_m = polar.requestedNearRadialStepM;
+    manifest.realized_near_radial_step_m = polar.realizedNearRadialStepM;
+    manifest.requested_outer_radial_step_max_m = ...
+        polar.requestedOuterRadialStepMaxM;
+    manifest.realized_outer_radial_step_m = polar.realizedOuterRadialStepM;
+    manifest.configured_outer_radial_layer_count = ...
+        polar.configuredOuterRadialLayerCount;
+    manifest.requested_outer_radial_layer_count = ...
+        polar.requestedOuterRadialLayerCount;
+    manifest.realized_outer_radial_layer_count = ...
+        polar.realizedOuterRadialLayerCount;
+    manifest.base_theta_count = polar.thetaNodeCount;
+    manifest.final_wall_theta_count = truncation.commonOuterThetaNodeCount;
+    manifest.top_connector_minimum_angle_deg = ...
+        truncation.topSurfaceDerived.oneToTwoConnectorAudit.minimumAngleDeg;
+    manifest.top_connector_maximum_aspect_ratio = ...
+        truncation.topSurfaceDerived.oneToTwoConnectorAudit.maximumAspectRatio;
+    manifest.bottom_connector_minimum_angle_deg = ...
+        truncation.bottomSurfaceDerived.oneToTwoConnectorAudit.minimumAngleDeg;
+    manifest.bottom_connector_maximum_aspect_ratio = ...
+        truncation.bottomSurfaceDerived.oneToTwoConnectorAudit.maximumAspectRatio;
+    manifest.design_canonical_sha256 = ...
+        string(definition.designCanonicalSHA256);
+    manifest.design_git_blob_id = string(definition.designGitBlobID);
+    manifest.design_v2_canonical_sha256 = ...
+        string(definition.designV2CanonicalSHA256);
+    manifest.design_v2_git_blob_id = string(definition.designV2GitBlobID);
+    manifest.design_v3_canonical_sha256 = ...
+        string(definition.designV3CanonicalSHA256);
+    manifest.design_v3_git_blob_id = string(definition.designV3GitBlobID);
+    bottomCore = domain.seabed.quality_controlled_bottom_core;
+    manifest.requested_bottom_core_radial_layer_count = ...
+        specification.level.bottomCoreRadialLayerCount;
+    manifest.realized_bottom_core_radial_layer_count = ...
+        bottomCore.realizedRadialLayerCount;
+    manifest.bottom_core_structured_panel_count = ...
+        bottomCore.totalStructuredCorePanelCount;
+    manifest.bottom_single_center_fan = ...
+        yes_no(bottomCore.singleCenterFan);
+    manifest.stale_result_reuse = "NO_MESH_REGENERATED_IN_UNIQUE_DIRECTORY";
+    manifestTable = struct2table(manifest);
+
+    components = {domain.body_list{1}, domain.fs, ...
+        domain.seabed, domain.farfield};
+    names = ["body"; "free_surface"; "bottom"; "farfield"];
+    rows = repmat(quality_row(), numel(components), 1);
+    for index = 1:numel(components)
+        [quality, ~] = phase32a.evaluate_component_quality( ...
+            components{index}, names(index), definition);
+        row = quality_row();
+        fields = fieldnames(quality);
+        for fieldIndex = 1:numel(fields)
+            row.(fields{fieldIndex}) = quality.(fields{fieldIndex});
+        end
+        row.mesh_level = specification.level.name;
+        row.mesh_level_index = specification.level.index;
+        row.omega_rad_s = specification.omegaRadPerS;
+        row.topology_mode = string(definition.topologyMode);
+        row.requested_near_radial_step_m = polar.requestedNearRadialStepM;
+        row.realized_near_radial_step_m = polar.realizedNearRadialStepM;
+        row.requested_outer_radial_step_max_m = ...
+            polar.requestedOuterRadialStepMaxM;
+        row.realized_outer_radial_step_m = polar.realizedOuterRadialStepM;
+        row.configured_outer_radial_layer_count = ...
+            polar.configuredOuterRadialLayerCount;
+        row.realized_outer_radial_layer_count = ...
+            polar.realizedOuterRadialLayerCount;
+        row.base_theta_count = polar.thetaNodeCount;
+        row.final_wall_theta_count = truncation.commonOuterThetaNodeCount;
+        row.requested_bottom_core_radial_layer_count = ...
+            specification.level.bottomCoreRadialLayerCount;
+        row.realized_bottom_core_radial_layer_count = ...
+            bottomCore.realizedRadialLayerCount;
+        row.bottom_core_structured_panel_count = ...
+            bottomCore.totalStructuredCorePanelCount;
+        row.transition_median_sqrt_area_m = ...
+            manifest.transition_median_sqrt_area_m;
+        row.manifold_status = manifest.manifold_status;
+        row.orientation_status = manifest.normal_orientation_status;
+        row.waterline_status = manifest.waterline_continuity_status;
+        row.self_intersection_status = manifest.self_intersection_status;
+        row.runtime_provenance = "PASS_CURRENT_WORKTREE";
+        rows(index) = row;
+    end
+    qualityTable = struct2table(rows);
+end
+
+function row = quality_row()
+    row = struct('mesh_level', "", 'mesh_level_index', 0, ...
+        'omega_rad_s', NaN, 'topology_mode', "", 'component', "", ...
+        'panel_count', 0, 'minimum_panel_area_m2', NaN, ...
+        'maximum_panel_area_m2', NaN, 'median_panel_area_m2', NaN, ...
+        'mean_sqrt_area_m', NaN, 'median_sqrt_area_m', NaN, ...
+        'minimum_edge_length_m', NaN, 'maximum_aspect_ratio', NaN, ...
+        'maximum_skewness_deg', NaN, 'minimum_corner_angle_deg', NaN, ...
+        'maximum_corner_angle_deg', NaN, 'maximum_warp_deg', NaN, ...
+        'poor_panel_count', 0, 'poor_panel_fraction', NaN, ...
+        'finite_positive_geometry', false, 'duplicate_panel_count', 0, ...
+        'component_gate_pass', false, 'component_gate_status', "NOT_RUN", ...
+        'requested_near_radial_step_m', NaN, ...
+        'realized_near_radial_step_m', NaN, ...
+        'requested_outer_radial_step_max_m', NaN, ...
+        'realized_outer_radial_step_m', NaN, ...
+        'configured_outer_radial_layer_count', 0, ...
+        'realized_outer_radial_layer_count', 0, ...
+        'base_theta_count', 0, 'final_wall_theta_count', 0, ...
+        'requested_bottom_core_radial_layer_count', 0, ...
+        'realized_bottom_core_radial_layer_count', 0, ...
+        'bottom_core_structured_panel_count', 0, ...
+        'transition_median_sqrt_area_m', NaN, ...
+        'manifold_status', "NOT_RUN", 'orientation_status', "NOT_RUN", ...
+        'waterline_status', "NOT_RUN", ...
+        'self_intersection_status', "NOT_RUN", ...
+        'runtime_provenance', "");
+end
+
+function text = yes_no(value)
+    if value
+        text = "YES";
+    else
+        text = "NO";
+    end
+end

+function specification = create_case(definition, level, omega, stageName)
+% CREATE_CASE Materialize one isolated QUALITY_CONTROLLED_V2 mesh/solve case.
+
+    validateattributes(omega, {'numeric'}, ...
+        {'scalar', 'real', 'positive', 'finite'});
+    runtimeDirectory = tempname(definition.artifactDirectory);
+    [created, message] = mkdir(runtimeDirectory);
+    assert(created, 'CRESTU:Phase32ARuntimeDirectory', ...
+        'Cannot create isolated runtime directory: %s (%s)', ...
+        runtimeDirectory, message);
+    [~, token] = fileparts(runtimeDirectory);
+    caseName = sprintf('P32A_%s_w%04d_%s', char(level.name), ...
+        round(1000 * omega), token);
+    bodyFile = fullfile(runtimeDirectory, [caseName, '_body.bmf']);
+    configFile = fullfile(runtimeDirectory, [caseName, '.cfg']);
+    baseline = read_config(definition.baselineConfig);
+    formal = read_config(definition.formalConfig);
+    [waveNumber, ~] = solve_wave_dispersion( ...
+        omega, baseline.grav, baseline.water_depth);
+    wavelengthM = 2 * pi / waveNumber; % [m]
+    innerRadiusM = baseline.fs.r_inner; % [m]
+    baselineTopRadiusM = innerRadiusM + ...
+        baseline.outer_truncation.topRadiusPerWavelength * wavelengthM;
+    baselineBottomRadiusM = innerRadiusM + ...
+        baseline.outer_truncation.bottomRadiusPerWavelength * wavelengthM;
+    targetTopRadiusM = definition.outerRadiusFactor * baselineTopRadiusM;
+    targetBottomRadiusM = definition.outerRadiusFactor * baselineBottomRadiusM;
+    qTop = (targetTopRadiusM - innerRadiusM) / wavelengthM;
+    qBottom = (targetBottomRadiusM - innerRadiusM) / wavelengthM;
+
+    generate_body_bmf(bodyFile, 2 * definition.sphereRadiusM, ...
+        baseline.isx, baseline.isy, level.bodyDivision);
+    write_runtime_config(configFile, caseName, bodyFile, omega, qTop, ...
+        qBottom, baseline, formal, level, definition.topologyMode);
+
+    physicalState = struct('analyticGeometry', 'hemisphere-radius-5m', ...
+        'sphereRadiusM', definition.sphereRadiusM, ...
+        'waterDepthM', baseline.water_depth, 'densityKgPerM3', baseline.rho, ...
+        'gravityMPerS2', baseline.grav, 'massProperties', baseline.mass_props, ...
+        'calculationModes', baseline.calc_modes, ...
+        'targetTopRadiusM', targetTopRadiusM, ...
+        'targetBottomRadiusM', targetBottomRadiusM, ...
+        'incidentHeadingDeg', 0.0, 'harmonicConvention', 'exp(+i*omega*t)');
+    controlState = struct('topologyMode', definition.topologyMode, ...
+        'level', level, 'omegaRadPerS', omega, 'qTop', qTop, ...
+        'qBottom', qBottom);
+    specification = struct('stage', string(stageName), 'level', level, ...
+        'omegaRadPerS', omega, 'runtimeDirectory', runtimeDirectory, ...
+        'caseName', caseName, 'bodyFile', bodyFile, 'configFile', configFile, ...
+        'waveNumberPerM', waveNumber, 'wavelengthM', wavelengthM, ...
+        'qTop', qTop, 'qBottom', qBottom, ...
+        'targetTopRadiusM', targetTopRadiusM, ...
+        'targetBottomRadiusM', targetBottomRadiusM, ...
+        'physicalHash', string(sha256_hash(jsonencode(physicalState))), ...
+        'controlHash', string(sha256_hash(jsonencode(controlState))), ...
+        'configRawSHA256', string(sha256_file_hash(configFile)), ...
+        'bodyFileRawSHA256', string(sha256_file_hash(bodyFile)), ...
+        'meshGenerationCommand', string(sprintf([ ...
+            'mode=%s;body=%d;FS=%d|%d;bottom=%d|%d;', ...
+            'wallNz=%d;theta=%d;outerFactor=1.5'], ...
+            definition.topologyMode, level.bodyDivision, ...
+            level.fsRadialCounts, level.bottomRadialCounts, ...
+            level.wallVerticalCount, level.wallThetaCount)));
+end
+
+function write_runtime_config(filename, caseName, bodyFile, omega, ...
+        qTop, qBottom, baseline, formal, level, topologyMode)
+    fileID = fopen(filename, 'w');
+    assert(fileID >= 0, 'CRESTU:Phase32AConfigOpen', ...
+        'Cannot create runtime configuration: %s', filename);
+    cleanup = onCleanup(@() fclose(fileID));
+    mass = baseline.mass_props(1);
+    inertia = mass.inertia;
+    inertiaTokens = [inertia(1, 1), inertia(2, 2), inertia(3, 3), ...
+        -inertia(1, 2), -inertia(1, 3), -inertia(2, 3)];
+    fprintf(fileID, 'PARA1: Project Case Name\n%s\n\n', caseName);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'PARA2: IPOTEN IFORCE IRAD IDIFF IDRIFT\n1 1 1 1 0\n\n');
+    fprintf(fileID, 'PARA3: FREQUENCY AND HEADING\n1 1 %.17g %.17g\n1\n0\n\n', ...
+        omega, omega);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'PARA4: ENVIRONMENT\n%.17g %.17g %.17g\n\n', ...
+        baseline.water_depth, baseline.grav, baseline.rho);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'PARA5: BODY\n1\n%d %s %.17g %.17g %.17g %.17g\n\n', ...
+        baseline.bodies(1).id, bodyFile, baseline.bodies(1).pos, ...
+        baseline.bodies(1).yaw);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'PARA6: MASS PROPERTIES\n%.17g %.17g %.17g %.17g\n', ...
+        mass.mass, mass.cg);
+    fprintf(fileID, '%.17g %.17g %.17g %.17g %.17g %.17g\n\n', ...
+        inertiaTokens);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'PARA7: MODES\n');
+    fprintf(fileID, '%d ', baseline.calc_modes);
+    fprintf(fileID, '\n\nPARA8: FULL DOMAIN\n%d %d\n\n', ...
+        baseline.isx, baseline.isy);
+    fprintf(fileID, ['PARA9: DOMAIN\n%.17g %d %d %.17g %.17g ', ...
+        '%.17g %d\n\n'], baseline.z_tol, baseline.fs.nr_near, ...
+        baseline.fs.nr_sponge, baseline.fs.r_inner, baseline.fs.r_outer, ...
+        baseline.fs.sponge_ratio, baseline.fs.nz_farfield);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'PARA10: SPONGE\n%.17g\n\n', baseline.fs.mu0);
+    fprintf(fileID, ['PARA11: FIXED OUTER-DOMAIN WATERLINE\n', ...
+        'OUTER_WATERLINE_MESH %s\n\n'], bodyFile);
+    fprintf(fileID, ['PARA12: PHASE2.2 OUTER TRUNCATION AUDIT\n', ...
+        'OUTER_TRUNCATION_Q %.17g %.17g\n\n'], qTop, qBottom);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'PARA13: PHASE3.2A QUALITY-CONTROLLED POLAR TOPOLOGY\n');
+    fprintf(fileID, 'FS_RADIAL_COUNTS %d %d\n', level.fsRadialCounts);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'BOTTOM_RADIAL_COUNTS %d %d\n', ...
+        level.bottomRadialCounts);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'BOTTOM_CORE_RADIAL_LAYERS %d\n', ...
+        level.bottomCoreRadialLayerCount);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'OUTER_VERTICAL_COUNT %d\n', level.wallVerticalCount);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'OUTER_THETA_COUNT %d\n', level.wallThetaCount);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'FS_OUTER_THETA_COUNT %d\n', level.fsThetaCount);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'BOTTOM_OUTER_THETA_COUNT %d\n', ...
+        level.bottomThetaCount);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'MESH_TRANSITION_RADIUS %.17g\n', baseline.fs.r_inner);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'SPONGE_START_RADIUS %.17g\n', baseline.fs.r_inner);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'POLAR_TOPOLOGY_MODE %s\n', topologyMode);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'RAW_DIAGNOSTICS 1\nPERSIST_FULL_PHI 0\n');
+    clear cleanup
+    assert(formal.wave.ndir >= 1 && any(abs(formal.wave.headings) <= eps), ...
+        'CRESTU:Phase32AHeadingConvention', ...
+        'Frozen formal configuration no longer includes heading 0.');
+end
diff --git a/Case_Wave/Case1_SingleSphere_Convergence/Phase3_2A_Artifacts/+phase32a/evaluate_component_quality.m b/Case_Wave/Case1_SingleSphere_Convergence/Phase3_2A_Artifacts/+phase32a/evaluate_component_quality.m

+function [summary, details] = evaluate_component_quality( ...
+        mesh, componentName, definition)
+% EVALUATE_COMPONENT_QUALITY Apply the frozen Phase 3.2 quality thresholds.
+
+    metrics = phase32a.evaluate_panels(mesh.vertices, mesh.normals, ...
+        definition.minimumEdgeLengthM);
+    panelCount = mesh.n_panels;
+    poor = false(panelCount, 1);
+    aspectLimit = zeros(panelCount, 1);
+    for panelIndex = 1:panelCount
+        radiusM = hypot(mesh.centers(panelIndex, 1), ...
+            mesh.centers(panelIndex, 2));
+        if componentName == "body"
+            aspectLimit(panelIndex) = definition.bodyMaximumAspectRatio;
+        elseif componentName == "farfield" || ...
+                radiusM > definition.meshTransitionRadiusM
+            aspectLimit(panelIndex) = definition.outerMaximumAspectRatio;
+        else
+            aspectLimit(panelIndex) = definition.nearMaximumAspectRatio;
+        end
+        poor(panelIndex) = ...
+            metrics.aspectRatio(panelIndex) > aspectLimit(panelIndex) || ...
+            metrics.skewnessDeg(panelIndex) > ...
+                definition.maximumSkewnessDeg || ...
+            metrics.minimumAngleDeg(panelIndex) < ...
+                definition.minimumCornerAngleDeg || ...
+            metrics.maximumAngleDeg(panelIndex) > ...
+                definition.maximumCornerAngleDeg || ...
+            metrics.warpDeg(panelIndex) > definition.maximumWarpDeg || ...
+            metrics.minimumEdgeLengthM(panelIndex) <= ...
+                definition.minimumEdgeLengthM;
+    end
+    finitePositive = all(isfinite(mesh.vertices), 'all') && ...
+        all(isfinite(mesh.centers), 'all') && ...
+        all(isfinite(mesh.normals), 'all') && ...
+        all(isfinite(metrics.areaM2)) && all(metrics.areaM2 > 0);
+    poorFraction = nnz(poor) / panelCount;
+    componentPass = finitePositive && poorFraction <= ...
+        definition.maximumPoorPanelFraction;
+    summary = struct('component', string(componentName), ...
+        'panel_count', panelCount, ...
+        'minimum_panel_area_m2', min(metrics.areaM2), ...
+        'maximum_panel_area_m2', max(metrics.areaM2), ...
+        'median_panel_area_m2', median(metrics.areaM2), ...
+        'mean_sqrt_area_m', mean(sqrt(metrics.areaM2)), ...
+        'median_sqrt_area_m', median(sqrt(metrics.areaM2)), ...
+        'minimum_edge_length_m', min(metrics.minimumEdgeLengthM), ...
+        'maximum_aspect_ratio', max(metrics.aspectRatio), ...
+        'maximum_skewness_deg', max(metrics.skewnessDeg), ...
+        'minimum_corner_angle_deg', min(metrics.minimumAngleDeg), ...
+        'maximum_corner_angle_deg', max(metrics.maximumAngleDeg), ...
+        'maximum_warp_deg', max(metrics.warpDeg), ...
+        'poor_panel_count', nnz(poor), ...
+        'poor_panel_fraction', poorFraction, ...
+        'finite_positive_geometry', finitePositive, ...
+        'duplicate_panel_count', metrics.duplicatePanelCount, ...
+        'component_gate_pass', componentPass, ...
+        'component_gate_status', pass_fail(componentPass));
+    details = table((1:panelCount)', metrics.isTriangle, ...
+        mesh.centers(:, 1), mesh.centers(:, 2), mesh.centers(:, 3), ...
+        metrics.areaM2, metrics.aspectRatio, metrics.minimumAngleDeg, ...
+        metrics.maximumAngleDeg, metrics.skewnessDeg, metrics.warpDeg, ...
+        metrics.minimumEdgeLengthM, aspectLimit, ...
+        metrics.orientationAlignment, poor, ...
+        'VariableNames', {'panel_id', 'is_triangle', 'center_x_m', ...
+        'center_y_m', 'center_z_m', 'area_m2', 'aspect_ratio', ...
+        'minimum_angle_deg', 'maximum_angle_deg', 'skewness_deg', ...
+        'warp_deg', 'minimum_edge_length_m', 'aspect_limit', ...
+        'orientation_alignment', 'is_poor'});
+end
+
+function text = pass_fail(value)
+    if value
+        text = "PASS";
+    else
+        text = "FAIL";
+    end
+end
diff --git a/Case_Wave/Case1_SingleSphere_Convergence/Phase3_2A_Artifacts/+phase32a/evaluate_isolation.m b/Case_Wave/Case1_SingleSphere_Convergence/Phase3_2A_Artifacts/+phase32a/evaluate_isolation.m

+function tableOut = evaluate_isolation(definition, omega, manifest, quality)
+% EVALUATE_ISOLATION Enforce v5 per-frequency coordinated refinement gates.
+
+    manifest = sortrows(manifest, 'mesh_level_index');
+    levelCount = numel(definition.levels);
+    countPass = height(manifest) == levelCount && ...
+        all(diff(manifest.body_panel_count) > 0) && ...
+        all(diff(manifest.free_surface_panel_count) > 0) && ...
+        all(diff(manifest.bottom_panel_count) > 0) && ...
+        all(diff(manifest.farfield_panel_count) > 0) && ...
+        all(diff(manifest.realized_outer_radial_layer_count) > 0) && ...
+        all(diff(manifest.base_theta_count) > 0) && ...
+        all(diff(manifest.final_wall_theta_count) > 0);
+    countPass = countPass && ...
+        all(diff(manifest.realized_bottom_core_radial_layer_count) > 0) && ...
+        all(diff(manifest.bottom_core_structured_panel_count) > 0);
+    hPass = true;
+    requestedRealizedPass = true;
+    componentNames = unique(quality.component, 'stable');
+    for index = 1:numel(componentNames)
+        rows = quality.component == componentNames(index);
+        componentRows = sortrows(quality(rows, :), 'mesh_level_index');
+        hPass = hPass && height(componentRows) == levelCount && ...
+            all(diff(componentRows.mean_sqrt_area_m) < 0) && ...
+            all(diff(componentRows.median_sqrt_area_m) < 0);
+        requestedRealizedPass = requestedRealizedPass && ...
+            all(componentRows.realized_near_radial_step_m <= ...
+            componentRows.requested_near_radial_step_m .* (1 + 1.0e-10)) && ...
+            all(componentRows.realized_outer_radial_step_m <= ...
+            componentRows.requested_outer_radial_step_max_m .* (1 + 1.0e-10));
+    end
+    fsRows = sortrows(quality(quality.component == "free_surface", :), ...
+        'mesh_level_index');
+    transitionPass = all(diff(fsRows.transition_median_sqrt_area_m) < 0);
+    extentPass = max(manifest.actual_top_outer_radius_m) - ...
+        min(manifest.actual_top_outer_radius_m) <= ...
+        definition.geometryToleranceM && ...
+        max(manifest.actual_bottom_outer_radius_m) - ...
+        min(manifest.actual_bottom_outer_radius_m) <= ...
+        definition.geometryToleranceM;
+    qualityPass = height(quality) == 4 * levelCount && ...
+        all(quality.component_gate_status == "PASS");
+    topologyPass = all(manifest.manifold_status == "PASS") && ...
+        all(manifest.normal_orientation_status == "PASS") && ...
+        all(manifest.waterline_continuity_status == "PASS") && ...
+        all(manifest.self_intersection_status == "PASS");
+    hashPass = numel(unique(manifest.combined_geometry_hash)) == levelCount;
+    sourcePass = numel(unique(manifest.production_source_hash)) == 1 && ...
+        numel(unique(manifest.design_canonical_sha256)) == 1 && ...
+        numel(unique(manifest.design_git_blob_id)) == 1 && ...
+        numel(unique(manifest.design_v2_canonical_sha256)) == 1 && ...
+        numel(unique(manifest.design_v2_git_blob_id)) == 1;
+    sourcePass = sourcePass && ...
+        numel(unique(manifest.design_v3_canonical_sha256)) == 1 && ...
+        numel(unique(manifest.design_v3_git_blob_id)) == 1;
+    noReuse = all(manifest.stale_result_reuse == ...
+        "NO_MESH_REGENERATED_IN_UNIQUE_DIRECTORY");
+    gatePass = countPass && hPass && requestedRealizedPass && ...
+        transitionPass && extentPass && qualityPass && topologyPass && ...
+        hashPass && sourcePass && noReuse;
+    tableOut = table(omega, levelCount, countPass, hPass, ...
+        requestedRealizedPass, transitionPass, extentPass, qualityPass, ...
+        topologyPass, hashPass, sourcePass, noReuse, pass_fail(gatePass), ...
+        'VariableNames', {'omega_rad_s', 'level_count', ...
+        'all_requested_integer_counts_strictly_increase', ...
+        'all_component_mean_median_h_strictly_decrease', ...
+        'realized_steps_meet_requested_maxima', ...
+        'transition_median_h_strictly_decreases', ...
+        'same_frequency_outer_extent_exact', ...
+        'all_component_quality_rows_pass', 'all_topology_contracts_pass', ...
+        'combined_hashes_distinct', 'source_and_design_fixed', ...
+        'no_stale_result_reuse', 'frequency_geometry_gate'});
+end
+
+function text = pass_fail(value)
+    if value
+        text = "PASS";
+    else
+        text = "FAIL";
+    end
+end

+function result = evaluate_panels(vertices, expectedNormals, toleranceM)
+% EVALUATE_PANELS Compute triangle-aware planar panel quality metrics.
+
+    arguments
+        vertices (:, 4, 3) double
+        expectedNormals (:, 3) double = zeros(0, 3)
+        toleranceM (1, 1) double {mustBePositive} = 1.0e-10
+    end
+    panelCount = size(vertices, 1);
+    assert(panelCount > 0 && all(isfinite(vertices), 'all'), ...
+        'CRESTU:Phase32AQualityVertices', ...
+        'Panel fixtures/meshes must contain finite vertices.');
+    if isempty(expectedNormals)
+        expectedNormals = NaN(panelCount, 3);
+    end
+    assert(size(expectedNormals, 1) == panelCount, ...
+        'CRESTU:Phase32AQualityNormalShape', ...
+        'Expected normals must have one row per panel.');
+
+    aspectRatio = zeros(panelCount, 1);
+    minimumAngleDeg = zeros(panelCount, 1);
+    maximumAngleDeg = zeros(panelCount, 1);
+    skewnessDeg = zeros(panelCount, 1);
+    warpDeg = zeros(panelCount, 1);
+    minimumEdgeLengthM = zeros(panelCount, 1);
+    areaM2 = zeros(panelCount, 1);
+    windingNormal = zeros(panelCount, 3);
+    orientationAlignment = NaN(panelCount, 1);
+    isTriangle = false(panelCount, 1);
+    unexpectedWithinPanelDuplicate = false(panelCount, 1);
+    panelKeys = strings(panelCount, 1);
+
+    for panelIndex = 1:panelCount
+        panel = reshape(vertices(panelIndex, :, :), 4, 3);
+        isTriangle(panelIndex) = ...
+            norm(panel(4, :) - panel(3, :)) <= toleranceM;
+        if isTriangle(panelIndex)
+            active = panel(1:3, :);
+        else
+            active = panel;
+        end
+        quantized = int64(round(active / toleranceM));
+        unexpectedWithinPanelDuplicate(panelIndex) = ...
+            size(unique(quantized, 'rows'), 1) ~= size(active, 1);
+        sortedVertices = sortrows(quantized);
+        panelKeys(panelIndex) = string(sprintf('%d,', sortedVertices.'));
+
+        vertexCount = size(active, 1);
+        next = [2:vertexCount, 1];
+        edgeVectors = active(next, :) - active;
+        edgeLengths = vecnorm(edgeVectors, 2, 2);
+        assert(all(edgeLengths > toleranceM), ...
+            'CRESTU:Phase32AQualityZeroEdge', ...
+            'A non-triangle panel contains a zero-length edge.');
+        minimumEdgeLengthM(panelIndex) = min(edgeLengths);
+        aspectRatio(panelIndex) = max(edgeLengths) / min(edgeLengths);
+
+        angles = zeros(vertexCount, 1);
+        for vertexIndex = 1:vertexCount
+            previousIndex = mod(vertexIndex - 2, vertexCount) + 1;
+            incoming = active(previousIndex, :) - active(vertexIndex, :);
+            outgoing = active(next(vertexIndex), :) - active(vertexIndex, :);
+            cosine = dot(incoming, outgoing) / ...
+                (norm(incoming) * norm(outgoing));
+            angles(vertexIndex) = acosd(max(-1, min(1, cosine)));
+        end
+        minimumAngleDeg(panelIndex) = min(angles);
+        maximumAngleDeg(panelIndex) = max(angles);
+        skewnessDeg(panelIndex) = max(abs(angles - 90));
+
+        areaVector = cross(panel(3, :) - panel(1, :), ...
+            panel(4, :) - panel(2, :));
+        areaMagnitude = norm(areaVector);
+        assert(areaMagnitude > 0, 'CRESTU:Phase32AQualityZeroArea', ...
+            'A panel has zero area.');
+        areaM2(panelIndex) = 0.5 * areaMagnitude;
+        windingNormal(panelIndex, :) = areaVector / areaMagnitude;
+        if all(isfinite(expectedNormals(panelIndex, :)))
+            expected = expectedNormals(panelIndex, :) / ...
+                norm(expectedNormals(panelIndex, :));
+            orientationAlignment(panelIndex) = dot( ...
+                windingNormal(panelIndex, :), expected);
+        end
+
+        if vertexCount == 4
+            firstNormal = cross(edgeVectors(1, :), edgeVectors(2, :));
+            secondNormal = cross(edgeVectors(3, :), edgeVectors(4, :));
+            cosine = dot(firstNormal, secondNormal) / ...
+                (norm(firstNormal) * norm(secondNormal));
+            warpDeg(panelIndex) = acosd(max(-1, min(1, cosine)));
+        end
+    end
+
+    duplicatePanelCount = panelCount - numel(unique(panelKeys));
+    result = struct('schemaVersion', 1, 'panelCount', panelCount, ...
+        'aspectRatio', aspectRatio, 'minimumAngleDeg', minimumAngleDeg, ...
+        'maximumAngleDeg', maximumAngleDeg, 'skewnessDeg', skewnessDeg, ...
+        'warpDeg', warpDeg, 'minimumEdgeLengthM', minimumEdgeLengthM, ...
+        'areaM2', areaM2, 'windingNormal', windingNormal, ...
+        'orientationAlignment', orientationAlignment, ...
+        'isTriangle', isTriangle, ...
+        'unexpectedWithinPanelDuplicate', unexpectedWithinPanelDuplicate, ...
+        'duplicatePanelCount', duplicatePanelCount, ...
+        'medianCharacteristicHM', median(sqrt(areaM2)), ...
+        'meanCharacteristicHM', mean(sqrt(areaM2)), ...
+        'totalAreaM2', sum(areaM2));
+end

+function levels = phase32a_levels()
+    template = struct('name', "", 'index', 0, 'eta', NaN, ...
+        'bodyDivision', 0, 'fsRadialCounts', [0, 0], ...
+        'bottomRadialCounts', [0, 0], 'wallVerticalCount', 0, ...
+        'wallThetaCount', 0, 'fsThetaCount', 0, ...
+        'bottomThetaCount', 0, 'bottomCoreRadialLayerCount', 0);
+    levels = repmat(template, 3, 1);
+    levels(1) = struct('name', "MESH_L1", 'index', 1, ...
+        'eta', 0.0707009403590185, 'bodyDivision', 4, ...
+        'fsRadialCounts', [4, 2], 'bottomRadialCounts', [4, 2], ...
+        'wallVerticalCount', 3, 'wallThetaCount', 64, ...
+        'fsThetaCount', 64, 'bottomThetaCount', 64, ...
+        'bottomCoreRadialLayerCount', 4);
+    levels(2) = struct('name', "MESH_L2", 'index', 2, ...
+        'eta', 0.0550177552570247, 'bodyDivision', 5, ...
+        'fsRadialCounts', [5, 3], 'bottomRadialCounts', [5, 3], ...
+        'wallVerticalCount', 4, 'wallThetaCount', 80, ...
+        'fsThetaCount', 80, 'bottomThetaCount', 80, ...
+        'bottomCoreRadialLayerCount', 7);
+    levels(3) = struct('name', "MESH_L3", 'index', 3, ...
+        'eta', 0.0412542094946932, 'bodyDivision', 7, ...
+        'fsRadialCounts', [6, 4], 'bottomRadialCounts', [6, 4], ...
+        'wallVerticalCount', 5, 'wallThetaCount', 112, ...
+        'fsThetaCount', 112, 'bottomThetaCount', 112, ...
+        'bottomCoreRadialLayerCount', 12);
+end
+
+function [output, status] = git_query(repositoryRoot, arguments)
+    [status, output] = system(sprintf('git -C "%s" %s', ...
+        repositoryRoot, arguments));
+end
+
+function value = same_path(firstPath, secondPath)
+    value = strcmpi(normalize_path(firstPath), normalize_path(secondPath));
+end
+
+function value = path_is_within(candidatePath, rootPath)
+    candidate = normalize_path(candidatePath);
+    root = normalize_path(rootPath);
+    value = strcmpi(candidate, root) || startsWith(lower(candidate), ...
+        [lower(root), filesep]);
+end
+
+function value = normalize_path(inputPath)
+    value = strrep(char(string(inputPath)), '/', filesep);
+    while numel(value) > 3 && endsWith(value, filesep)
+        value(end) = [];
+    end
+end

+# CRESTU Phase 3.2A Polar Topology Design Decision（冻结）
+
+冻结时间：2026-09-01；在实现 `QUALITY_CONTROLLED_V2`、查看 endpoint prototype 结果之前。
+
+## 1. Fail-before 结论
+
+独立 analytic fixtures 6/6 PASS；新 evaluator 从 mesh 逐行复算 blocked-v4 的 72/72 quality rows，得到相同的 36 PASS / 36 FAIL。因此 `METRIC_BUG=NO`。
+
+- FS `AR=122.201862` 位于 MESH_L2、omega=0.5、base polar ring 8、sector 40、panel 320。该面板内侧两个顶点被 radial grading/legacy elliptic relaxation 拉回约 `r=20 m`，外侧两个顶点在 `r=350 m`。这是 base FS radial scheduler/relaxation 的真实几何缺陷。
+- bottom 的 18–19 deg minimum angle 位于 outer-truncation 的 zipper triangles，不是 single-center fan。当前 bottom producer 已使用 adaptive 5-block structured core。
+- omega=0.5 的 combined transition median h 为 L1/L2/L3=`35.6223/12.2430/15.5471 m`。L2 在 `r=20 m` 附近产生异常小面元，改变合并样本的中位数排序；不是 CSV 统计错误。
+
+这些是多个已确认机制，不合并伪装成一个唯一 root cause。总体仍标记：
+
+```ini
+BASE_POLAR_TOPOLOGY_CLASSIFICATION = PRIMARY_DESIGN_LIMITATION
+UNIQUE_ROOT_CAUSE = NO
+```
+
+## 2. Mode contract
+
+```ini
+POLAR_TOPOLOGY_MODE = LEGACY
+POLAR_TOPOLOGY_MODE = QUALITY_CONTROLLED_V2
+DEFAULT_MODE = LEGACY
+```
+
+未显式提供 `POLAR_TOPOLOGY_MODE` 的 production/config/历史 runner 必须继续使用 `LEGACY`，并保持 5 个冻结 q-only 1.5R component/combined hashes 完全一致。新模式只在 PARA13 显式请求时启用。
+
+## 3. v5 coordinated targets
+
+解析半球半径 `R=5 m`，mesh transition radius `Rt=20 m`。各级沿用 body divisions 4/5/7，并由 body waterline 精确给出 `N=32/40/56` 个 inner nodes。FS inner ring 与 body waterline逐点完全一致。
+
+| quantity | L1 | L2 | L3 |
+|---|---:|---:|---:|
+| body division | 4 | 5 | 7 |
+| inner/base theta count N | 32 | 40 | 56 |
+| final wall theta count 2N | 64 | 80 | 112 |
+| near radial layers | 4 | 5 | 6 |
+| configured minimum outer radial layers | 2 | 3 | 4 |
+| wall vertical control | 3 | 4 | 5 |
+| inner chord target [m] | 0.9801714033 | 0.7845909573 | 0.5607044724 |
+| requested near radial step [m] | 3.75 | 3.00 | 2.50 |
+| chord at Rt [m] | 3.9206856132 | 3.1383638291 | 2.2428178895 |
+| maximum requested outer radial step [m] | 31.3654849055 | 25.1069106329 | 17.9425431159 |
+
+定义：
+
+```text
+h_theta_inner = 2*R*sin(pi/N)
+dr_near = (Rt-R)/N_near
+h_theta_transition = 2*Rt*sin(pi/N)
+dr_outer_requested_max = 8*h_theta_transition
+N_outer_layers = max(N_outer_configured,
+                     ceil((R_legacy-Rt)/dr_outer_requested_max))
+```
+
+requested refinement ratios：near `r21=1.25, r32=1.20`；inner/transition tangential `r21≈1.2492, r32≈1.3992`。所有 realized counts 必须严格 L1<L2<L3；所有 realized component mean/median h 必须严格 L1>L2>L3。requested/realized h、ratio 和 integer counts 必须写入 endpoint/v5 evidence。
+
+## 4. FS QUALITY_CONTROLLED_V2
+
+1. 使用解析 annular O-grid；不执行 smoothing/elliptic relaxation。
+2. 从 body waterline 到 `Rt` 使用 N 个等角节点和预声明 near radial layers。
+3. 从 `Rt` 到 frequency-local legacy radius 使用 N 个等角节点；outer radial layers 由上述 requested maximum step 计算。
+4. base outer ring 保持 N nodes。outer truncation 只允许一个预定义 1:2 connector，把 N 变为 2N；禁止 arbitrary zipper。
+5. 1:2 connector 对每个 inner sector 使用固定三角模板，transition gap：
+
+```text
+dr_1to2 = min(0.5*legacy_ring_chord, realized_outer_radial_step)
+```
+
+6. connector 后到 final 1.5R ring 全部为 equal-count 2N quads；outer ring 与 FARFIELD wall top �愮偣鐩稿悓銆�
+
+## 5. Bottom QUALITY_CONTROLLED_V2
+
+1. 涓嶅紩鍏� single-center fan銆�
+2. 淇濈暀宸插瓨鍦ㄧ殑 finite adaptive 5-block structured core锛屼絾蹇呴』鐙珛閫氳繃 35 deg/AR/warp gate銆�
+3. core 澶栦娇鐢ㄧ敱 QUALITY_CONTROLLED_V2 FS 澶嶅埗骞朵笅绉诲埌 seabed 鐨� annular rings銆�
+4. outer truncation 浣跨敤涓� FS 鐩稿悓鐨勯瀹氫箟 1:2 connector锛涙渶缁� 2N ring 涓� wall bottom 閫愮偣鐩稿悓銆�
+
+## 6. Hard gates锛堜笉鍙橈級
+
+- minimum corner angle `>=35 deg`锛沚ody AR `<=4`锛沶ear AR `<=4.5`锛沷uter/transition/wall AR `<=12`锛泂kewness `<=55 deg`锛泈arp `<=8 deg`锛沺oor fraction `<=5%`銆�
+- endpoint prototype 浠� omega=0.5/2.0 脳 L1/L2/L3锛岃姹� 24/24 component rows PASS銆乼ransition median h 涓ユ牸涓嬮檷銆佸洓鍖哄煙 count 鍧囧彉鍖栥€乷uter extent exact銆乵anifold/orientation/intersection/waterline PASS銆�
+- endpoint 鏈� PASS 涓嶅緱杩愯鍏朵綑 12 cases銆�
+- v5 鍏ㄩ噺瑕佹眰 72/72 component rows PASS锛涘彧鏈夋鍚庢墠鍏佽 omega=1.5 鐨勪袱涓湁闄� BEM bridge cases銆�
+- legacy q-only exact regression 蹇呴』濮嬬粓 5/5 PASS銆�
+
+## 7. Production modification boundary
+
+鍏佽淇敼锛歝onfig mode parser/manifest銆丗S/bottom mesh generation 鐨勬樉寮忔柊 mode銆乪xplicit new-mode 1:2 horizontal connector銆乧ache/source fingerprint 鍜� Phase 3.2A harness銆�
+
+绂佹淇敼锛歳adiation/diffraction RHS銆乼raction銆乷uter ABC operator銆乻ource orientation銆丄/B extraction銆丷AO equation銆乄AMIT reference銆佷换浣� quality threshold銆俙LEGACY` 鏁板€艰矾寰勫繀椤讳繚鎸� exact銆�

+# Phase 3.2A Topology Design Decision v3锛堝喕缁擄級
+
+鍐荤粨鏃堕棿锛�2026-09-02锛涘湪瀹炵幇 bottom-core schedule 鍜屾煡鐪嬪叾 endpoint/v5 结果之前。
+
+原始 design、connector-gap v2 design、initial/gap-v2 FAIL evidence 全部保留。gap-v2 的 omega=1.0 唯一失败仍是 bottom median h：`5.439254 → 5.750831 → 4.075027 m`。quality、mean h、connector quality、counts、transition h、topology 和 extent 全 PASS。
+
+Population audit：
+
+- L2 structured 5-block core median h=`0.481 m`，不是 quality blocker；
+- L2 overall median 落在 connector population minimum h=`5.750831 m`；
+- 继续缩小 connector gap 会消耗 35 deg angle margin，因此不再改变 connector geometry。
+
+## 显式 bottom core radial schedule
+
+新增仅 QUALITY_CONTROLLED_V2 使用的 named control：
+
+```ini
+BOTTOM_CORE_RADIAL_LAYERS = 4  # L1
+BOTTOM_CORE_RADIAL_LAYERS = 7  # L2
+BOTTOM_CORE_RADIAL_LAYERS = 12 # L3
+```
+
+`Nx=Ny=N_waterline/4` 保持不变，因此 waterline/core/annulus boundary nodes 与 segment counts 完全不变。只增加四个 structured side blocks 的径向层数 `Nr`；central patch 仍为 `Nx×Ny`，没有 single-center fan。
+
+| level | N waterline | Nx=Ny | Nr requested | side panels `4*Nx*Nr` | central panels `Nx*Ny` | total core panels |
+|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
+| L1 | 32 | 8 | 4 | 128 | 64 | 192 |
+| L2 | 40 | 10 | 7 | 280 | 100 | 380 |
+| L3 | 56 | 14 | 12 | 672 | 196 | 868 |
+
+该 schedule 明确、单调、满足边界 integer contract，不改变 outer annulus、connector、wall、body 或 physics。缺省/LEGACY mode 不读取该 control，继续使用原 `Nr=max(3,round((Nx+Ny)*0.25))`。
+
+必须重跑 legacy 5/5 exact、六个 endpoint 和完整18-case v5。所有原 hard gates 不变。
+
+```ini
+THRESHOLD_RELAXATION = NO
+WATERLINE_NODE_CHANGE = NO
+CENTRAL_PATCH_TOPOLOGY_CHANGE = NO
+SINGLE_CENTER_FAN = NO
+PHYSICS_CHANGE = NO
+```

+function summary = Run_Phase3_2A_BEM_Bridge_Smoke()
+% RUN_PHASE3_2A_BEM_BRIDGE_SMOKE Run exactly two omega=1.5 clean solves.
+
+    %% Stage 1: Verify geometry prerequisites and frozen legacy reference
+
+    definition = phase32a.initialize();
+    v5Cells = readcell(fullfile(definition.artifactDirectory, ...
+        'Phase3_2A_V5_Mesh_Status.csv'));
+    names = string(v5Cells(2:end, 1));
+    values = string(v5Cells(2:end, 2));
+    v5Row = names == "V5_MESH_GEOMETRY_GATE";
+    assert(nnz(v5Row) == 1 && values(v5Row) == "PASS", ...
+        'CRESTU:Phase32ABridgeGeometryPrerequisite', ...
+        'BEM bridge requires the complete v5 mesh geometry gate PASS.');
+    legacyReference = readtable(fullfile(definition.convergenceDirectory, ...
+        'Phase3_1_Artifacts', 'Phase3_1_Formal_PFull_Frequency_Sweep.csv'), ...
+        'TextType', 'string');
+    referenceRow = abs(legacyReference.omega_rad_s - 1.5) <= 64 * eps(1.5);
+    assert(nnz(referenceRow) == 1, 'CRESTU:Phase32ABridgeLegacyReference', ...
+        'Frozen Phase 3.1 omega=1.5 formal reference row is missing.');
+    reference = legacyReference(referenceRow, :);
+    outputFile = fullfile(definition.artifactDirectory, ...
+        'Phase3_2A_BEM_Bridge_Smoke.csv');
+    records = repmat(empty_record(), 0, 1);
+
+    %% Stage 2: Run and enforce the LEGACY frozen regression first
+
+    legacyCase = create_legacy_case(definition, 1.5);
+    legacyCleanup = onCleanup(@() phase32.remove_runtime( ...
+        definition, legacyCase.runtimeDirectory));
+    legacyRecord = run_one_case(definition, legacyCase, "LEGACY");
+    legacyRecord = bind_legacy_regression(legacyRecord, reference);
+    records(end + 1, 1) = legacyRecord;
+    writetable(struct2table(records), outputFile);
+    clear legacyCleanup
+    assert(legacyRecord.solve_status == "PASS" && ...
+        legacyRecord.legacy_regression_status == "PASS", ...
+        'CRESTU:Phase32ABridgeLegacyFailure', ...
+        'LEGACY omega=1.5 clean regression failed; new-mode solve is forbidden.');
+
+    %% Stage 3: Run one QUALITY_CONTROLLED_V2 L3 clean solve
+
+    newCase = phase32a.create_case(definition, definition.levels(3), ...
+        1.5, 'phase3_2a_bem_bridge_smoke');
+    newCleanup = onCleanup(@() phase32.remove_runtime( ...
+        definition, newCase.runtimeDirectory));
+    newRecord = run_one_case(definition, newCase, "QUALITY_CONTROLLED_V2");
+    newRecord.legacy_regression_status = "NOT_APPLICABLE_NEW_MODE";
+    records(end + 1, 1) = newRecord;
+    writetable(struct2table(records), outputFile);
+    clear newCleanup
+
+    ready = all([records.solve_status] == "PASS") && ...
+        legacyRecord.legacy_regression_status == "PASS";
+    summary = struct('schemaVersion', 1, 'caseCount', numel(records), ...
+        'legacyStatus', char(legacyRecord.solve_status), ...
+        'legacyRegressionStatus', char(legacyRecord.legacy_regression_status), ...
+        'qualityControlledStatus', char(newRecord.solve_status), ...
+        'readyForPhase32BSentinel', ready);
+    fprintf('[RESULT] BEM_BRIDGE_LEGACY = %s\n', legacyRecord.solve_status);
+    fprintf('[RESULT] BEM_BRIDGE_QUALITY_CONTROLLED_V2 = %s\n', ...
+        newRecord.solve_status);
+    fprintf('[RESULT] READY_FOR_PHASE3_2B_SENTINEL = %s\n', yes_no(ready));
+    assert(ready, 'CRESTU:Phase32ABridgeOverallFailure', ...
+        'The limited Phase 3.2A BEM bridge smoke did not pass.');
+end
+
+function specification = create_legacy_case(definition, omega)
+% CREATE_LEGACY_CASE Reproduce the frozen Phase 3.1 formal q-only case.
+
+    runtimeDirectory = tempname(definition.artifactDirectory);
+    mkdir(runtimeDirectory);
+    [~, token] = fileparts(runtimeDirectory);
+    caseName = sprintf('P32A_LegacyBridge_w%04d_%s', ...
+        round(1000 * omega), token);
+    bodyFile = fullfile(runtimeDirectory, [caseName, '_body.bmf']);
+    configFile = fullfile(runtimeDirectory, [caseName, '.cfg']);
+    baseline = read_config(definition.baselineConfig);
+    generate_body_bmf(bodyFile, 2 * definition.sphereRadiusM, ...
+        baseline.isx, baseline.isy, 7);
+    write_legacy_config(configFile, caseName, bodyFile, omega, baseline);
+    specification = struct('runtimeDirectory', runtimeDirectory, ...
+        'configFile', configFile, 'bodyFile', bodyFile, ...
+        'caseName', caseName, 'omegaRadPerS', omega, ...
+        'configRawSHA256', string(sha256_file_hash(configFile)));
+end
+
+function write_legacy_config(filename, caseName, bodyFile, omega, baseline)
+    fileID = fopen(filename, 'w');
+    assert(fileID >= 0, 'CRESTU:Phase32ABridgeLegacyConfigOpen', ...
+        'Cannot create legacy bridge configuration.');
+    cleanup = onCleanup(@() fclose(fileID));
+    mass = baseline.mass_props(1);
+    inertia = mass.inertia;
+    inertiaTokens = [inertia(1, 1), inertia(2, 2), inertia(3, 3), ...
+        -inertia(1, 2), -inertia(1, 3), -inertia(2, 3)];
+    fprintf(fileID, 'PARA1: Project Case Name\n%s\n\n', caseName);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'PARA2: IPOTEN IFORCE IRAD IDIFF IDRIFT\n1 1 1 1 0\n\n');
+    fprintf(fileID, 'PARA3: FREQUENCY AND HEADING\n1 1 %.17g %.17g\n1\n0\n\n', ...
+        omega, omega);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'PARA4: ENVIRONMENT\n%.17g %.17g %.17g\n\n', ...
+        baseline.water_depth, baseline.grav, baseline.rho);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'PARA5: BODY\n1\n%d %s %.17g %.17g %.17g %.17g\n\n', ...
+        baseline.bodies(1).id, bodyFile, baseline.bodies(1).pos, ...
+        baseline.bodies(1).yaw);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'PARA6: MASS PROPERTIES\n%.17g %.17g %.17g %.17g\n', ...
+        mass.mass, mass.cg);
+    fprintf(fileID, '%.17g %.17g %.17g %.17g %.17g %.17g\n\n', ...
+        inertiaTokens);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'PARA7: MODES\n');
+    fprintf(fileID, '%d ', baseline.calc_modes);
+    fprintf(fileID, '\n\nPARA8: FULL DOMAIN\n%d %d\n\n', ...
+        baseline.isx, baseline.isy);
+    fprintf(fileID, ['PARA9: DOMAIN\n%.17g %d %d %.17g %.17g ', ...
+        '%.17g %d\n\n'], baseline.z_tol, baseline.fs.nr_near, ...
+        baseline.fs.nr_sponge, baseline.fs.r_inner, baseline.fs.r_outer, ...
+        baseline.fs.sponge_ratio, baseline.fs.nz_farfield);
+    fprintf(fileID, 'PARA10: SPONGE\n%.17g\n\n', baseline.fs.mu0);
+    fprintf(fileID, ['PARA11: FIXED OUTER-DOMAIN WATERLINE\n', ...
+        'OUTER_WATERLINE_MESH %s\n\n'], bodyFile);
+    fprintf(fileID, ['PARA12: PHASE2.2 OUTER TRUNCATION AUDIT\n', ...
+        'OUTER_TRUNCATION_Q 2 1.5\n\n']);
+    fprintf(fileID, ['PARA13: LEGACY BRIDGE DIAGNOSTICS\n', ...
+        'RAW_DIAGNOSTICS 1\nPERSIST_FULL_PHI 0\n']);
+    clear cleanup
+end
+
+function record = run_one_case(definition, specification, mode)
+% RUN_ONE_CASE Execute one clean production solve and extract direct gates.
+
+    record = empty_record();
+    record.topology_mode = string(mode);
+    record.omega_rad_s = specification.omegaRadPerS;
+    record.config_raw_sha256 = specification.configRawSHA256;
+    timer = tic;
+    try
+        results = run_frequency_domain_case(specification.configFile, ...
+            'skipPhysicalDiagnostics', true);
+        record.total_runtime_s = toc(timer);
+        assert_runtime_scope(definition, specification, results);
+        loaded = load(results.potential_cache_file, 'pot_cache');
+        entry = loaded.pot_cache.entries(1);
+        diagnostics = entry.solver_diagnostics;
+        conditioning = cellfun(@(item) item.conditioning, diagnostics, ...
+            'UniformOutput', false);
+        assembly = entry.assembly_info;
+        record.matrix_assembly_status = "PASS";
+        record.bem_unknown_count = entry.mesh_audit.bemUnknownCount;
+        record.raw_rcond = min(cellfun(@(item) ...
+            item.rawRcondEstimate, conditioning));
+        record.scaled_rcond = min(cellfun(@(item) ...
+            item.scaledRcondEstimate, conditioning));
+        record.radiation_linear_relative_residual = max(cellfun(@(item) ...
+            item.radiationRelativeResidual, diagnostics));
+        record.diffraction_linear_relative_residual = max(cellfun(@(item) ...
+            item.diffractionRelativeResidual, diagnostics));
+        record.radiation_algebraic_relative_residual = max(cellfun(@(item) ...
+            item.radiation.relativeResidualFrobeniusNorm, diagnostics));
+        record.diffraction_algebraic_relative_residual = max(cellfun(@(item) ...
+            item.diffraction.relativeResidualFrobeniusNorm, diagnostics));
+        record.maximum_abs_outer_ntheta = max(cellfun(@(item) ...
+            item.outer_absorbing_boundary.maximumAbsTangentialNormal, assembly));
+        record.maximum_outer_column_residual = max(cellfun(@(item) ...
+            item.outer_source_column_audit.reconstructionRelativeResidual, assembly));
+        record.assembly_runtime_s = sum(cellfun(@(item) ...
+            item.elapsed_seconds, assembly));
+        record.A33_kg = results.added_mass(3, 3, 1);
+        record.B33_kg_s = results.damping(3, 3, 1);
+        excitation = results.excitation(3, 1, 1);
+        response = results.rao.complex(3, 1, 1);
+        denominator = results.rao.dynamic_matrix(3, 3, 1);
+        record.F3_real_N_per_m = real(excitation);
+        record.F3_imag_N_per_m = imag(excitation);
+        record.RAO3_real_m_per_m = real(response);
+        record.RAO3_imag_m_per_m = imag(response);
+        record.D3_real_N_per_m = real(denominator);
+        record.D3_imag_N_per_m = imag(denominator);
+        record.D3_abs_N_per_m = abs(denominator);
+        audit = results.audit.frequencyEntries(1);
+        record.cache_status = string(audit.cacheStatus);
+        record.body_geometry_hash = string(entry.mesh_audit.body.hash);
+        record.free_surface_geometry_hash = ...
+            string(entry.mesh_audit.freeSurface.hash);
+        record.bottom_geometry_hash = string(entry.mesh_audit.bottom.hash);
+        record.farfield_geometry_hash = ...
+            string(entry.mesh_audit.outerBoundary.hash);
+        record.combined_geometry_hash = string(entry.mesh_audit.geometry.hash);
+        record.operator_hash = string(entry.phase2_2_control_manifest. ...
+            effective.boundaryOperatorStateSHA256);
+        record.production_source_hash = string(results.audit.codeVersion.fingerprint);
+        finiteValues = [record.raw_rcond, record.scaled_rcond, ...
+            record.radiation_linear_relative_residual, ...
+            record.diffraction_linear_relative_residual, ...
+            record.radiation_algebraic_relative_residual, ...
+            record.diffraction_algebraic_relative_residual, ...
+            record.A33_kg, record.B33_kg_s, real(excitation), ...
+            imag(excitation), real(response), imag(response), ...
+            real(denominator), imag(denominator)];
+        maximumResidual = max([ ...
+            record.radiation_linear_relative_residual, ...
+            record.diffraction_linear_relative_residual, ...
+            record.radiation_algebraic_relative_residual, ...
+            record.diffraction_algebraic_relative_residual]);
+        pass = all(isfinite(finiteValues)) && ...
+            record.raw_rcond >= 1.0e-12 && ...
+            record.scaled_rcond >= 1.0e-12 && ...
+            maximumResidual <= 1.0e-10 && ...
+            record.maximum_abs_outer_ntheta <= 1.0e-10 && ...
+            record.maximum_outer_column_residual <= 1.0e-14 && ...
+            lower(record.cache_status) == "miss";
+        record.solve_status = pass_fail(pass);
+    catch exception
+        record.total_runtime_s = toc(timer);
+        record.solve_status = "FAIL";
+        record.error_identifier = string(exception.identifier);
+        record.error_message = string(exception.message);
+    end
+end
+
+function record = bind_legacy_regression(record, reference)
+    referenceExcitation = complex(reference.F3_real_N_per_m, ...
+        reference.F3_imag_N_per_m);
+    referenceResponse = complex(reference.RAO3_real_m_per_m, ...
+        reference.RAO3_imag_m_per_m);
+    actualExcitation = complex(record.F3_real_N_per_m, record.F3_imag_N_per_m);
+    actualResponse = complex(record.RAO3_real_m_per_m, ...
+        record.RAO3_imag_m_per_m);
+    differences = [relative_difference(record.A33_kg, reference.A33_kg); ...
+        relative_difference(record.B33_kg_s, reference.B33_kg_s); ...
+        relative_difference(actualExcitation, referenceExcitation); ...
+        relative_difference(actualResponse, referenceResponse)];
+    record.legacy_maximum_response_relative_difference = max(differences);
+    geometryMatch = record.body_geometry_hash == ...
+        reference.body_geometry_hash && ...
+        record.free_surface_geometry_hash == ...
+        reference.free_surface_geometry_hash && ...
+        record.bottom_geometry_hash == reference.bottom_geometry_hash && ...
+        record.farfield_geometry_hash == reference.farfield_geometry_hash && ...
+        record.combined_geometry_hash == reference.combined_geometry_hash;
+    record.legacy_geometry_exact_hash_match = geometryMatch;
+    pass = geometryMatch && max(differences) <= 1.0e-10;
+    record.legacy_regression_status = pass_fail(pass);
+end
+
+function value = relative_difference(actual, expected)
+    value = abs(actual - expected) / max([abs(actual), abs(expected), eps]);
+end
+
+function assert_runtime_scope(definition, specification, results)
+    paths = [string(specification.configFile); string(specification.bodyFile); ...
+        string(results.config.files.potential_cache); ...
+        string(results.config.files.results); ...
+        string(results.audit.meshAuditFile)];
+    prefix = lower(string(specification.runtimeDirectory) + filesep);
+    for index = 1:numel(paths)
+        normalized = lower(strrep(paths(index), '/', filesep));
+        assert(startsWith(normalized, prefix) && ...
+            ~contains(normalized, lower(definition.originalRepository)), ...
+            'CRESTU:Phase32ABridgeRuntimeScope', ...
+            'A BEM bridge path escaped the isolated current-worktree case.');
+    end
+end
+
+function record = empty_record()
+    record = struct('topology_mode', "", 'omega_rad_s', NaN, ...
+        'matrix_assembly_status', "NOT_RUN", 'bem_unknown_count', 0, ...
+        'raw_rcond', NaN, 'scaled_rcond', NaN, ...
+        'radiation_linear_relative_residual', NaN, ...
+        'diffraction_linear_relative_residual', NaN, ...
+        'radiation_algebraic_relative_residual', NaN, ...
+        'diffraction_algebraic_relative_residual', NaN, ...
+        'maximum_abs_outer_ntheta', NaN, ...
+        'maximum_outer_column_residual', NaN, ...
+        'A33_kg', NaN, 'B33_kg_s', NaN, ...
+        'F3_real_N_per_m', NaN, 'F3_imag_N_per_m', NaN, ...
+        'RAO3_real_m_per_m', NaN, 'RAO3_imag_m_per_m', NaN, ...
+        'D3_real_N_per_m', NaN, 'D3_imag_N_per_m', NaN, ...
+        'D3_abs_N_per_m', NaN, 'cache_status', "NOT_RUN", ...
+        'body_geometry_hash', "", 'free_surface_geometry_hash', "", ...
+        'bottom_geometry_hash', "", 'farfield_geometry_hash', "", ...
+        'combined_geometry_hash', "", 'operator_hash', "", ...
+        'production_source_hash', "", 'config_raw_sha256', "", ...
+        'assembly_runtime_s', NaN, 'total_runtime_s', NaN, ...
+        'legacy_geometry_exact_hash_match', false, ...
+        'legacy_maximum_response_relative_difference', NaN, ...
+        'legacy_regression_status', "NOT_RUN", 'solve_status', "NOT_RUN", ...
+        'error_identifier', "", 'error_message', "");
+end
+
+function text = pass_fail(value)
+    if value
+        text = "PASS";
+    else
+        text = "FAIL";
+    end
+end
+
+function text = yes_no(value)
+    if value
+        text = 'YES';
+    else
+        text = 'NO';
+    end
+end

+function summary = Run_Phase3_2A_Endpoint_Prototype()
+% RUN_PHASE3_2A_ENDPOINT_PROTOTYPE Audit only omega=0.5/2.0 mesh endpoints.
+
+    %% Stage 1: Verify frozen design and legacy exact regression evidence
+
+    definition = phase32a.initialize();
+    legacyFile = fullfile(definition.convergenceDirectory, ...
+        'Phase3_2_Artifacts', 'Phase3_2_Legacy_Mesh_Regression.csv');
+    legacy = readtable(legacyFile, 'TextType', 'string');
+    assert(height(legacy) == 5 && all(legacy.exact_hash_regression), ...
+        'CRESTU:Phase32AEndpointLegacyRegression', ...
+        'Endpoint prototype requires 5/5 exact LEGACY geometry regression.');
+    writetable(legacy, fullfile(definition.artifactDirectory, ...
+        'Phase3_2A_Legacy_Regression.csv'));
+    legacyAuditDefinition = phase32.definition();
+
+    manifestTable = table();
+    qualityTable = table();
+    isolationTable = table();
+    manifestFile = fullfile(definition.artifactDirectory, ...
+        'Phase3_2A_Endpoint_Prototype_Manifest.csv');
+    qualityFile = fullfile(definition.artifactDirectory, ...
+        'Phase3_2A_Endpoint_Prototype_Quality.csv');
+    isolationFile = fullfile(definition.artifactDirectory, ...
+        'Phase3_2A_Endpoint_Prototype_Control_Isolation.csv');
+
+    %% Stage 2: Run three levels at low endpoint before high endpoint
+
+    frequencies = definition.endpointFrequenciesRadPerS;
+    for frequencyIndex = 1:numel(frequencies)
+        omega = frequencies(frequencyIndex); % [rad/s]
+        for levelIndex = 1:numel(definition.levels)
+            level = definition.levels(levelIndex);
+            fprintf('[INFO] Phase 3.2A endpoint | %.6g rad/s | %s\n', ...
+                omega, level.name);
+            specification = phase32a.create_case(definition, level, omega, ...
+                'phase3_2a_endpoint_prototype');
+            cleanup = onCleanup(@() phase32.remove_runtime( ...
+                definition, specification.runtimeDirectory));
+            timer = tic;
+            try
+                domain = build_bmf_domain(specification.configFile);
+                generationRuntimeS = toc(timer);
+                assert_runtime_scope(definition, specification, domain);
+                [manifest, ~] = phase32.audit_domain( ...
+                    legacyAuditDefinition, specification, domain, ...
+                    generationRuntimeS);
+                manifest.phase32a_design_v2_canonical_sha256 = ...
+                    string(definition.designV2CanonicalSHA256);
+                manifest.phase32a_design_v2_git_blob_id = ...
+                    string(definition.designV2GitBlobID);
+                manifest.phase32a_design_v3_canonical_sha256 = ...
+                    string(definition.designV3CanonicalSHA256);
+                manifest.phase32a_design_v3_git_blob_id = ...
+                    string(definition.designV3GitBlobID);
+                manifest.connector_gap_coefficient = 0.4;
+                bottomCore = domain.seabed.quality_controlled_bottom_core;
+                manifest.requested_bottom_core_radial_layer_count = ...
+                    level.bottomCoreRadialLayerCount;
+                manifest.realized_bottom_core_radial_layer_count = ...
+                    bottomCore.realizedRadialLayerCount;
+                manifest.bottom_core_structured_panel_count = ...
+                    bottomCore.totalStructuredCorePanelCount;
+                currentManifest = struct2table(manifest);
+                currentQuality = evaluate_endpoint_components( ...
+                    definition, specification, domain, manifest);
+                manifestTable = append_table(manifestTable, currentManifest);
+                qualityTable = append_table(qualityTable, currentQuality);
+                writetable(manifestTable, manifestFile);
+                writetable(qualityTable, qualityFile);
+            catch exception
+                failure = endpoint_failure_row(specification, exception);
+                qualityTable = append_table(qualityTable, failure);
+                writetable(qualityTable, qualityFile);
+                clear cleanup
+                rethrow(exception)
+            end
+            clear cleanup
+        end
+
+        currentManifest = manifestTable( ...
+            abs(manifestTable.omega_rad_s - omega) <= 64 * eps(max(1, omega)), :);
+        currentQuality = qualityTable( ...
+            abs(qualityTable.omega_rad_s - omega) <= 64 * eps(max(1, omega)), :);
+        currentIsolation = evaluate_frequency_isolation( ...
+            definition, omega, currentManifest, currentQuality);
+        isolationTable = append_table(isolationTable, currentIsolation);
+        writetable(isolationTable, isolationFile);
+        assert(currentIsolation.endpoint_frequency_gate == "PASS", ...
+            'CRESTU:Phase32AEndpointFrequencyGate', ...
+            'Endpoint %.6g rad/s failed; high/expanded cases are forbidden.', omega);
+    end
+
+    %% Stage 3: Enforce the complete six-case endpoint hard gate
+
+    qualityPass = height(qualityTable) == 24 && ...
+        all(qualityTable.component_gate_status == "PASS");
+    topologyPass = all(manifestTable.manifold_status == "PASS") && ...
+        all(manifestTable.normal_orientation_status == "PASS") && ...
+        all(manifestTable.waterline_continuity_status == "PASS") && ...
+        all(manifestTable.self_intersection_status == "PASS");
+    isolationPass = height(isolationTable) == 2 && ...
+        all(isolationTable.endpoint_frequency_gate == "PASS");
+    overallPass = qualityPass && topologyPass && isolationPass;
+    statusName = ["ENDPOINT_COMPONENT_QUALITY"; "ENDPOINT_TOPOLOGY"; ...
+        "ENDPOINT_CONTROL_ISOLATION"; "LEGACY_Q_ONLY_REGRESSION"; ...
+        "ENDPOINT_PROTOTYPE_OVERALL"];
+    statusValue = [pass_fail(qualityPass); pass_fail(topologyPass); ...
+        pass_fail(isolationPass); "PASS_EXACT_HASH_5_OF_5"; ...
+        pass_fail(overallPass)];
+    evidence = ["Phase3_2A_Endpoint_Prototype_Quality.csv"; ...
+        "Phase3_2A_Endpoint_Prototype_Manifest.csv"; ...
+        "Phase3_2A_Endpoint_Prototype_Control_Isolation.csv"; ...
+        "Phase3_2A_Legacy_Regression.csv"; ...
+        "all endpoint hard gates"];
+    writetable(table(statusName, statusValue, evidence), ...
+        fullfile(definition.artifactDirectory, ...
+        'Phase3_2A_Endpoint_Prototype_Status.csv'));
+    summary = struct('schemaVersion', 1, 'meshOnlyCaseCount', 6, ...
+        'componentQualityRowCount', height(qualityTable), ...
+        'componentQualityPassCount', nnz( ...
+            qualityTable.component_gate_status == "PASS"), ...
+        'qualityPass', qualityPass, 'topologyPass', topologyPass, ...
+        'isolationPass', isolationPass, 'overallPass', overallPass);
+    fprintf('[RESULT] PHASE3_2A_ENDPOINT_PROTOTYPE = %s\n', ...
+        pass_fail(overallPass));
+    assert(overallPass, 'CRESTU:Phase32AEndpointOverallGate', ...
+        'Phase 3.2A endpoint prototype did not pass every hard gate.');
+end
+
+function tableOut = evaluate_endpoint_components( ...
+        definition, specification, domain, manifest)
+    components = {domain.body_list{1}, domain.fs, ...
+        domain.seabed, domain.farfield};
+    names = ["body"; "free_surface"; "bottom"; "farfield"];
+    polar = domain.fs.quality_controlled_polar;
+    truncation = domain.cfg.outer_truncation.effective;
+    rows = repmat(endpoint_quality_row(), numel(components), 1);
+    for index = 1:numel(components)
+        [quality, ~] = phase32a.evaluate_component_quality( ...
+            components{index}, names(index), definition);
+        rows(index) = endpoint_quality_row();
+        fields = fieldnames(quality);
+        for fieldIndex = 1:numel(fields)
+            rows(index).(fields{fieldIndex}) = quality.(fields{fieldIndex});
+        end
+        rows(index).mesh_level = specification.level.name;
+        rows(index).mesh_level_index = specification.level.index;
+        rows(index).omega_rad_s = specification.omegaRadPerS;
+        rows(index).topology_mode = string(definition.topologyMode);
+        rows(index).design_v2_canonical_sha256 = ...
+            string(definition.designV2CanonicalSHA256);
+        rows(index).design_v2_git_blob_id = string(definition.designV2GitBlobID);
+        rows(index).design_v3_canonical_sha256 = ...
+            string(definition.designV3CanonicalSHA256);
+        rows(index).design_v3_git_blob_id = string(definition.designV3GitBlobID);
+        rows(index).connector_gap_coefficient = 0.4;
+        rows(index).requested_near_radial_step_m = ...
+            polar.requestedNearRadialStepM;
+        rows(index).realized_near_radial_step_m = ...
+            polar.realizedNearRadialStepM;
+        rows(index).requested_outer_radial_step_max_m = ...
+            polar.requestedOuterRadialStepMaxM;
+        rows(index).realized_outer_radial_step_m = ...
+            polar.realizedOuterRadialStepM;
+        rows(index).configured_outer_radial_layer_count = ...
+            polar.configuredOuterRadialLayerCount;
+        rows(index).realized_outer_radial_layer_count = ...
+            polar.realizedOuterRadialLayerCount;
+        rows(index).base_theta_count = polar.thetaNodeCount;
+        rows(index).final_wall_theta_count = ...
+            truncation.commonOuterThetaNodeCount;
+        bottomCore = domain.seabed.quality_controlled_bottom_core;
+        rows(index).requested_bottom_core_radial_layer_count = ...
+            specification.level.bottomCoreRadialLayerCount;
+        rows(index).realized_bottom_core_radial_layer_count = ...
+            bottomCore.realizedRadialLayerCount;
+        rows(index).bottom_core_structured_panel_count = ...
+            bottomCore.totalStructuredCorePanelCount;
+        rows(index).top_connector_minimum_angle_deg = ...
+            truncation.topSurfaceDerived.oneToTwoConnectorAudit.minimumAngleDeg;
+        rows(index).top_connector_maximum_aspect_ratio = ...
+            truncation.topSurfaceDerived.oneToTwoConnectorAudit.maximumAspectRatio;
+        rows(index).bottom_connector_minimum_angle_deg = ...
+            truncation.bottomSurfaceDerived.oneToTwoConnectorAudit.minimumAngleDeg;
+        rows(index).bottom_connector_maximum_aspect_ratio = ...
+            truncation.bottomSurfaceDerived.oneToTwoConnectorAudit.maximumAspectRatio;
+        rows(index).transition_median_sqrt_area_m = ...
+            manifest.transition_median_sqrt_area_m;
+        rows(index).manifold_status = manifest.manifold_status;
+        rows(index).orientation_status = manifest.normal_orientation_status;
+        rows(index).waterline_status = manifest.waterline_continuity_status;
+        rows(index).self_intersection_status = ...
+            manifest.self_intersection_status;
+        rows(index).actual_top_outer_radius_m = ...
+            manifest.actual_top_outer_radius_m;
+        rows(index).actual_bottom_outer_radius_m = ...
+            manifest.actual_bottom_outer_radius_m;
+        rows(index).combined_geometry_hash = manifest.combined_geometry_hash;
+        rows(index).production_source_hash = manifest.production_source_hash;
+        rows(index).runtime_provenance = "PASS_CURRENT_WORKTREE";
+        rows(index).error_identifier = "";
+        rows(index).error_message = "";
+    end
+    tableOut = struct2table(rows);
+end
+
+function tableOut = evaluate_frequency_isolation( ...
+        definition, omega, manifest, quality)
+    manifest = sortrows(manifest, 'mesh_level_index');
+    quality = sortrows(quality, {'component', 'mesh_level_index'});
+    levelCount = numel(definition.levels);
+    countPass = height(manifest) == levelCount && ...
+        all(diff(manifest.body_panel_count) > 0) && ...
+        all(diff(manifest.free_surface_panel_count) > 0) && ...
+        all(diff(manifest.bottom_panel_count) > 0) && ...
+        all(diff(manifest.farfield_panel_count) > 0);
+    hPass = true;
+    componentNames = unique(quality.component, 'stable');
+    for index = 1:numel(componentNames)
+        rows = quality.component == componentNames(index);
+        componentRows = sortrows(quality(rows, :), 'mesh_level_index');
+        hPass = hPass && height(componentRows) == levelCount && ...
+            all(diff(componentRows.mean_sqrt_area_m) < 0) && ...
+            all(diff(componentRows.median_sqrt_area_m) < 0);
+    end
+    transitionRows = sortrows(quality(quality.component == "free_surface", :), ...
+        'mesh_level_index');
+    transitionPass = all(diff( ...
+        transitionRows.transition_median_sqrt_area_m) < 0);
+    extentPass = max(manifest.actual_top_outer_radius_m) - ...
+        min(manifest.actual_top_outer_radius_m) <= ...
+        definition.geometryToleranceM && ...
+        max(manifest.actual_bottom_outer_radius_m) - ...
+        min(manifest.actual_bottom_outer_radius_m) <= ...
+        definition.geometryToleranceM;
+    qualityPass = height(quality) == 4 * levelCount && ...
+        all(quality.component_gate_status == "PASS");
+    topologyPass = all(manifest.manifold_status == "PASS") && ...
+        all(manifest.normal_orientation_status == "PASS") && ...
+        all(manifest.waterline_continuity_status == "PASS") && ...
+        all(manifest.self_intersection_status == "PASS");
+    hashPass = numel(unique(manifest.combined_geometry_hash)) == levelCount;
+    gatePass = countPass && hPass && transitionPass && extentPass && ...
+        qualityPass && topologyPass && hashPass;
+    tableOut = table(omega, levelCount, countPass, hPass, transitionPass, ...
+        extentPass, qualityPass, topologyPass, hashPass, pass_fail(gatePass), ...
+        'VariableNames', {'omega_rad_s', 'level_count', ...
+        'all_region_counts_strictly_increase', ...
+        'all_component_mean_median_h_strictly_decrease', ...
+        'transition_median_h_strictly_decreases', ...
+        'same_frequency_outer_extent_exact', ...
+        'all_component_quality_rows_pass', 'all_topology_contracts_pass', ...
+        'combined_hashes_distinct', 'endpoint_frequency_gate'});
+end
+
+function row = endpoint_quality_row()
+    row = struct('mesh_level', "", 'mesh_level_index', 0, ...
+        'omega_rad_s', NaN, 'topology_mode', "", ...
+        'design_v2_canonical_sha256', "", 'design_v2_git_blob_id', "", ...
+        'design_v3_canonical_sha256', "", 'design_v3_git_blob_id', "", ...
+        'connector_gap_coefficient', NaN, 'component', "", ...
+        'panel_count', 0, 'minimum_panel_area_m2', NaN, ...
+        'maximum_panel_area_m2', NaN, 'median_panel_area_m2', NaN, ...
+        'mean_sqrt_area_m', NaN, 'median_sqrt_area_m', NaN, ...
+        'minimum_edge_length_m', NaN, 'maximum_aspect_ratio', NaN, ...
+        'maximum_skewness_deg', NaN, 'minimum_corner_angle_deg', NaN, ...
+        'maximum_corner_angle_deg', NaN, 'maximum_warp_deg', NaN, ...
+        'poor_panel_count', 0, 'poor_panel_fraction', NaN, ...
+        'finite_positive_geometry', false, 'duplicate_panel_count', 0, ...
+        'component_gate_pass', false, 'component_gate_status', "NOT_RUN", ...
+        'requested_near_radial_step_m', NaN, ...
+        'realized_near_radial_step_m', NaN, ...
+        'requested_outer_radial_step_max_m', NaN, ...
+        'realized_outer_radial_step_m', NaN, ...
+        'configured_outer_radial_layer_count', 0, ...
+        'realized_outer_radial_layer_count', 0, ...
+        'base_theta_count', 0, 'final_wall_theta_count', 0, ...
+        'requested_bottom_core_radial_layer_count', 0, ...
+        'realized_bottom_core_radial_layer_count', 0, ...
+        'bottom_core_structured_panel_count', 0, ...
+        'top_connector_minimum_angle_deg', NaN, ...
+        'top_connector_maximum_aspect_ratio', NaN, ...
+        'bottom_connector_minimum_angle_deg', NaN, ...
+        'bottom_connector_maximum_aspect_ratio', NaN, ...
+        'transition_median_sqrt_area_m', NaN, ...
+        'manifold_status', "NOT_RUN", 'orientation_status', "NOT_RUN", ...
+        'waterline_status', "NOT_RUN", ...
+        'self_intersection_status', "NOT_RUN", ...
+        'actual_top_outer_radius_m', NaN, ...
+        'actual_bottom_outer_radius_m', NaN, ...
+        'combined_geometry_hash', "", 'production_source_hash', "", ...
+        'runtime_provenance', "", 'error_identifier', "", ...
+        'error_message', "");
+end
+
+function tableOut = endpoint_failure_row(specification, exception)
+    row = endpoint_quality_row();
+    row.mesh_level = specification.level.name;
+    row.mesh_level_index = specification.level.index;
+    row.omega_rad_s = specification.omegaRadPerS;
+    row.topology_mode = "QUALITY_CONTROLLED_V2";
+    row.component = "case_build";
+    row.component_gate_status = "FAIL";
+    row.error_identifier = string(exception.identifier);
+    row.error_message = string(exception.message);
+    tableOut = struct2table(row);
+end
+
+function combined = append_table(combined, current)
+    if isempty(combined)
+        combined = current;
+    else
+        combined = [combined; current];
+    end
+end
+
+function assert_runtime_scope(definition, specification, domain)
+    paths = [string(specification.configFile); string(specification.bodyFile); ...
+        string(domain.cfg.files.fs); string(domain.cfg.files.seabed); ...
+        string(domain.cfg.files.farfield)];
+    runtimePrefix = lower(string(specification.runtimeDirectory) + filesep);
+    for index = 1:numel(paths)
+        normalized = lower(strrep(paths(index), '/', filesep));
+        assert(startsWith(normalized, runtimePrefix) && ...
+            ~contains(normalized, lower(definition.originalRepository)), ...
+            'CRESTU:Phase32AEndpointRuntimeScope', ...
+            'Endpoint prototype path escaped the isolated current-worktree case.');
+    end
+end
+
+function text = pass_fail(value)
+    if value
+        text = "PASS";
+    else
+        text = "FAIL";
+    end
+end

+function summary = Run_Phase3_2A_Generator_Audit()
+% RUN_PHASE3_2A_GENERATOR_AUDIT Locate fail-before panels and producer chain.
+
+    %% Stage 1: Freeze the read-only call-chain interpretation
+
+    definition = phase32a.initialize();
+    callChain = build_call_chain();
+    writetable(callChain, fullfile(definition.artifactDirectory, ...
+        'Phase3_2A_Generator_Call_Chain.csv'));
+
+    %% Stage 2: Rebuild only low/high endpoint v4 meshes
+
+    v4 = phase32.definition();
+    frequencies = [0.5, 2.0]; % [rad/s]
+    levels = v4.levels;
+    diagnosticRows = repmat(empty_panel_record(), 0, 1);
+    transitionRows = repmat(empty_transition_record(), 0, 1);
+    diagnosticFile = fullfile(definition.artifactDirectory, ...
+        'Phase3_2A_FailBefore_Panel_Diagnostics.csv');
+    transitionFile = fullfile(definition.artifactDirectory, ...
+        'Phase3_2A_Transition_H_Diagnostics.csv');
+
+    caseCount = numel(frequencies) * numel(levels);
+    completed = 0;
+    for frequencyIndex = 1:numel(frequencies)
+        omega = frequencies(frequencyIndex); % [rad/s]
+        for levelIndex = 1:numel(levels)
+            level = levels(levelIndex);
+            completed = completed + 1;
+            fprintf('[INFO] Generator audit %d/%d | %s | %.6g rad/s\n', ...
+                completed, caseCount, level.name, omega);
+            specification = phase32.create_case(v4, level, omega, ...
+                'phase3_2a_generator_audit');
+            cleanup = onCleanup(@() phase32.remove_runtime( ...
+                v4, specification.runtimeDirectory));
+            domain = build_bmf_domain(specification.configFile);
+            assert_runtime_scope(definition, specification, domain);
+            truncation = domain.cfg.outer_truncation.effective;
+            waterlineCount = domain.outer_waterlines{1}.n_pts;
+            components = {domain.body_list{1}, domain.fs, ...
+                domain.seabed, domain.farfield};
+            names = ["body"; "free_surface"; "bottom"; "farfield"];
+            for componentIndex = 1:numel(components)
+                mesh = components{componentIndex};
+                [~, details] = phase32a.evaluate_component_quality( ...
+                    mesh, names(componentIndex), definition);
+                [~, aspectOrder] = sort(details.aspect_ratio, 'descend');
+                [~, angleOrder] = sort(details.minimum_angle_deg, 'ascend');
+                selectionCount = min(5, mesh.n_panels);
+                for rankIndex = 1:selectionCount
+                    panelIndex = aspectOrder(rankIndex);
+                    diagnosticRows(end + 1, 1) = panel_record( ...
+                        definition, specification, truncation, ...
+                        names(componentIndex), mesh, details, panelIndex, ...
+                        "WORST_ASPECT_RATIO", rankIndex, waterlineCount); %#ok<AGROW>
+                    panelIndex = angleOrder(rankIndex);
+                    diagnosticRows(end + 1, 1) = panel_record( ...
+                        definition, specification, truncation, ...
+                        names(componentIndex), mesh, details, panelIndex, ...
+                        "WORST_MINIMUM_ANGLE", rankIndex, waterlineCount); %#ok<AGROW>
+                end
+            end
+            transitionRows = append_transition_records(transitionRows, ...
+                specification, domain, truncation);
+            writetable(struct2table(diagnosticRows), diagnosticFile);
+            writetable(struct2table(transitionRows), transitionFile);
+            clear cleanup
+        end
+    end
+
+    %% Stage 3: Classify evidence without overclaiming a unique root cause
+
+    diagnostics = struct2table(diagnosticRows);
+    transitions = struct2table(transitionRows);
+    fsLow = diagnostics.component == "free_surface" & ...
+        diagnostics.omega_rad_s == 0.5;
+    bottomHigh = diagnostics.component == "bottom" & ...
+        diagnostics.omega_rad_s == 2.0;
+    fsWorstAspect = max(diagnostics.aspect_ratio(fsLow));
+    bottomMinimumAngle = min(diagnostics.minimum_angle_deg(bottomHigh));
+    bottomWorst = diagnostics(bottomHigh & ...
+        diagnostics.minimum_angle_deg == bottomMinimumAngle, :);
+    bottomSingleCenterFan = any(bottomWorst.generator_region == ...
+        "BOTTOM_SINGLE_CENTER_FAN");
+    summary = struct('schemaVersion', 1, ...
+        'selectedCaseCount', caseCount, ...
+        'diagnosticPanelRowCount', height(diagnostics), ...
+        'transitionSummaryRowCount', height(transitions), ...
+        'fsOmega0p5MaximumAspectRatio', fsWorstAspect, ...
+        'bottomOmega2MinimumAngleDeg', bottomMinimumAngle, ...
+        'bottomMinimumAngleFromSingleCenterFan', bottomSingleCenterFan, ...
+        'metricBug', false, ...
+        'classification', 'PRIMARY_DESIGN_LIMITATION', ...
+        'uniqueRootCauseConfirmed', false);
+    fprintf('[RESULT] FS_OMEGA_0P5_MAX_ASPECT_RATIO = %.9g\n', ...
+        fsWorstAspect);
+    fprintf('[RESULT] BOTTOM_OMEGA_2_MIN_ANGLE_DEG = %.9g\n', ...
+        bottomMinimumAngle);
+    fprintf('[RESULT] BOTTOM_SINGLE_CENTER_FAN = %s\n', ...
+        yes_no(bottomSingleCenterFan));
+    fprintf('[RESULT] METRIC_BUG = NO\n');
+    fprintf('[RESULT] ROOT_CAUSE = NOT_YET_UNIQUE\n');
+end
+
+function tableOut = build_call_chain()
+    functionName = ["read_config/parse_phase2_2_controls"; ...
+        "build_bmf_domain"; "extract_waterline"; ...
+        "generate_free_surface_bmf"; ...
+        "get_phase2_2_bottom_mesh_inputs"; "generate_seabed_mesh"; ...
+        "apply_rankine_outer_truncation/choose_endpoint_theta_count"; ...
+        "apply_rankine_outer_truncation/prepare_horizontal_surface"; ...
+        "apply_rankine_outer_truncation/gradual_ring_count"; ...
+        "apply_rankine_outer_truncation/zipper_annulus"; ...
+        "apply_rankine_outer_truncation/build_frustum_wall"; ...
+        "generate_farfield_mesh"];
+    relativeFile = ["Source Code/1.Input/read_config.m"; ...
+        "Source Code/2.Mesh/build_bmf_domain.m"; ...
+        "Source Code/2.Mesh/extract_waterline.m"; ...
+        "Source Code/2.Mesh/generate_free_surface_bmf.m"; ...
+        "Source Code/2.Mesh/get_phase2_2_bottom_mesh_inputs.m"; ...
+        "Source Code/2.Mesh/generate_seabed_mesh.m"; ...
+        repmat("Source Code/2.Mesh/apply_rankine_outer_truncation.m", 5, 1); ...
+        "Source Code/2.Mesh/generate_farfield_mesh.m"];
+    role = ["configuration producer"; "domain orchestrator"; ...
+        "body-waterline producer"; "FS base polar producer"; ...
+        "bottom control adapter"; "bottom ring/core producer"; ...
+        "endpoint count scheduler"; "radial/count scheduler"; ...
+        "integer count scheduler"; "transition connector"; ...
+        "wall top/bottom ring consumer"; "legacy wall producer"];
+    primaryInput = ["PARA13 named controls"; "validated cfg/body mesh"; ...
+        "body vertices/normals/z_tol"; ...
+        "waterline nodes,nr_near,nr_sponge,Rinner,Rout,sponge_ratio"; ...
+        "bottomRadialCounts,FS reference"; ...
+        "waterline,bottom cfg,mirrored FS ring"; ...
+        "radius,chord target,explicit/common count"; ...
+        "legacy/final radius,radial target,theta counts"; ...
+        "legacy/final count,layer index"; ...
+        "two ordered circular rings"; ...
+        "top/bottom rings,depth,slant target"; ...
+        "waterline nodes,Rout,Nz"];
+    outputContract = ["explicit controls or legacy defaults"; ...
+        "body/FS/bottom/wall merged domain"; ...
+        "ordered CCW closed ring with body node count"; ...
+        "equal-count radial quad rings; inner ring exactly body waterline"; ...
+        "independent bottom radial cfg/reference FS"; ...
+        "mirrored outer ring plus adaptive 5-block structured infill"; ...
+        "multiple-of-4 endpoint count; held-or-finer spacing"; ...
+        "horizontal annulus ending at common wall count"; ...
+        "multiple-of-4 monotone intermediate counts"; ...
+        "conforming quad/triangle panels; no T-junction"; ...
+        "equal-count structured FARFIELD frustum"; ...
+        "equal waterline sector count at top/bottom"];
+    integerRounding = ["positive integer validation"; "none"; "none"; ...
+        "nr counts exact; no theta change in base producer"; ...
+        "counts copied exactly"; ...
+        "corner indices/Nx/Ny/Nr use round"; ...
+        "round-to-multiple-4 or ceil-to-finer"; ...
+        "ceil(extension/radialTarget)"; ...
+        "4*round(interpolated/4)"; ...
+        "fraction-order merge"; "round/ceil vertical layers"; ...
+        "Nz exact; theta inherited from waterline"];
+    symmetryConstraint = ["theta controls multiple of four"; ...
+        "active truncation limited to full-domain single body"; ...
+        "open/closed waterline follows ISX/ISY"; ...
+        "n_theta equals waterline nodes"; ...
+        "inherits cfg symmetry"; "full-domain producer"; ...
+        "count multiple of four"; "full-domain active truncation"; ...
+        "count multiple of four"; "ordered closed rings"; ...
+        "common equal count"; "waterline sector count"];
+    tableOut = table(functionName, relativeFile, role, primaryInput, ...
+        outputContract, integerRounding, symmetryConstraint);
+end
